भारत सरकार, श्रम मंत्रालय, नई दिल्ली के प्रासांगिक आदेश दिनांक 23.02.2012 के जरिये निर्देश विवाद, औद्योगिक विवाद 


अधिनियम, 1947 (जिसे आगे “अधिनियम” से सम्बोधित किया जावेगा) की धारा 10(1)[घ) एवं उपधारा 2(क) के अन्तर्गत इस न्यायाधिकरण को 
अधिनिर्णयार्थ निम्नानुसार प्राप्त हुआ। 


"Whether the action of the management of M/s Manglam Cement Limited, Morak, Kota in teminating the 

services of Shri Bheru S/o Puri Lal w.e.f.6/1/2005 is legal and justified? What relief the workman in entitled 

to?" 
2-उक्‍्त विवाद, न्यायाधिकरण में रेफर होने पर पंजीबद्ध कर पक्षकारों को उपस्थिति बाबत नोटिस जारी किए गए। नोटिस की पालना में प्रार्थी श्रमिक 
द्वारा उपस्थित होकर स्टेटमेन्ट ऑफ क्लेम न्‍्यायाधिकरण के समक्ष प्रस्तुत कर संक्षिप्ततः यह कथन किया गया है कि उसे अप्रार्थी ने जून, 1983 में 
स्थायी रूप से नौकरी पर नियुक्त किया। weit अमिक उस समय से ही प्रबंधक मंगलम सीमेंट fe, रामगंजमण्डी के यहां भली प्रकार एवं सुचारू रूप 
से कर्तव्यनिष्ठा से अपनी ड्यूटी कर रहा था। श्रमिक आरंभ से ही अनुशासन में रहकर तथा अपनी ड्यूटी के प्रति एवं प्रबंधक वर्ग के हित को ध्यान 
में रखते हुए कार्य कर रहा था। प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा दि. 14.12.2004 को प्रार्थी श्रमिक को आरोप पत्र दिया गया तथा प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा बिना विधिवत 
जांच किए झूठी कार्यवाही की झूठी रिपोर्ट तैयार करके प्रार्थी श्रमिक को दि. 06.01.2005 को सेवा से पृथक कर दिया। प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा जांच 
अधिकारी नियुक्ति किया गया, जिसके द्वारा जांच की कार्यवाही की गई है वह प्रबंधक वर्ग के दबाव में रहकर निष्पक्ष कार्यवाही नहीं करके श्रमिक के 
विरूद्ध कार्यवाही की गई है। निष्पक्ष जांच करने के लिए प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा स्वतंत्र अधिकारी द्वारा जांच करवाई जानी चाहिए थी जो प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा 
नहीं की गई है अतः श्रमिक के विरूद्ध जो जांच की गई है वह निष्पक्ष नहीं है। प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा श्रमिक को दि. 14.12.2004 के द्वारा जो आरोप पत्र 
दिए गए है वह आरोप पत्र झूठ पर आधारित है। आरोप पत्र में सब तथ्य मनगढ़ंत और गलत है। WT AH को इन आरोपों की कतई जानकारी 
नहीं दी गई तथा प्रार्थी श्रमिक को दि. 14.12.2004 को जो नोटिस दिया गया है वह नोटिस फर्जी है उस पर प्रार्थी श्रमिक के हस्ताक्षर नहीं है तथा 
प्रार्थी अमिक ने किसी भी नोटिस पर हस्ताक्षर नहीं किए है। प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा किसी अन्य व्यक्ति के हस्ताक्षर well अमिक के नाम से करवाए गए हैं। 
प्रार्थी श्रमिक के हस्ताक्षर जो नोटिस पर किए गए है उन हस्ताक्षरों का मिलान हस्ताक्षर विशेषज्ञ से कराए जाए। प्रार्थी श्रमिक जो खर्चा हो होगा वह 
अदा करने को तैयार है। प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा प्रार्थी के अनुशासनात्मक कार्यवाही करने लिए जो विवरण दि. 04.09.1983 से 15.12.2004 तक प्रार्थी श्रमिक 
के विरुद्ध प्रस्तुत किया है वह कतई गलत है तथा गलत तथ्यों पर आधारित है। उसमें सही विवरण प्रस्तुत नहीं किया गया है। यह सारी कार्यवाही 
प्रबंधक वर्ग के अधीन रहकर तैयार कर प्रस्तुत की गई है। उक्त कार्यवाही के प्रार्थी श्रमिक के हस्ताक्षर नहीं कराए है जबकी प्रत्येक गैर हाजरी पर 
श्रमिक के हस्ताक्षर होना अंत्यंत आवश्यक है। दिनांक 01.12.2004 से 15.12.2004 तक प्रार्थी श्रमिक की पत्नी की तबीयत बहुत ज्यादा खराब होने 
के कारण प्रार्थी श्रमिक दि. 01.12.2004 से 15.12.2004 तक अवकाश पर रहा था। Vat अवकाश की जांच कार्यवाही के लिए दि. 15.12.2004 को ही 
जांच अधिकारी की नियुक्ति प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा दी गई तथा जांच करते समय प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा कोई निष्पक्ष एवं स्वतंत्र गवाह नहीं रखा गया। जांच 
करते समय जांच अधिकारी कम्पनी के ही कर्मचारी थे जबकि जांच अधिकारी कम्पनी के बाहर के होने चाहिए थे। जांच करते समय सुनील कुमार 
टाइम कीपर एवं पीएस दाधीच विभाग इंचार्ज मौजूद थे। जांच कार्यवाही में प्रार्थी अ्रमिक की अनुपस्थिति के बारे में चेतावनी दी गई तथा निलंबित 
किया। यह दोनों पत्र प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा प्रस्तुत नहीं किए है तथा दि. 14.12.2004 को जांच करने के लिए जो ओदश प्रबंधक द्वारा दिया गया है उस 
आदेश दि. 15.12.2004 की प्रति प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा प्रस्तुत नहीं की गई है। जिसके द्वारा जांच अधिकारी की नियुक्ति की गई है। इसके अलावा सुनील 
कुमार टाइम कीपर तथा पीएस erie विभाग इंचार्ज के बयान लिए गए है इन बयानों पर प्रार्थी श्रमिक के हस्ताक्षर नहीं है। well afte के जाली 
हस्ताक्षर कराए गए है। प्रार्थी श्रमिक के इन हस्ताक्षरों की भी हस्ताक्षर विशेषज्ञ से जांच कराना चाहिए जिसमें यह स्पष्ट हो सके कि हस्ताक्षर वास्तव 
में प्रार्थी श्रमिक के हैं। weit ata के समक्ष कोई कार्यवाही नहीं की गई है सारी कार्यवाही गलत है। प्रार्थी श्रमिक के स्वगर्गीय पिता श्री पूरीलाल 
की 20 बीघा काश्त की कृषि योग्य भूमि मंगलम सीमेंट फैक्ट्री द्वारा लेने के एवज में यह समझौता पूर्व विधायक श्री हरिश शर्मा एवं अन्य गणमान्य

था। यदि किसी कारण वश प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा प्रार्थी ate को सेवा में लेने को तैयार नहीं होते है तो प्रार्थी श्रमिक को वर्ष 200 से प्रार्थी की आयु

3-अप्रार्थी ने जवाब स्टेटमेंट ऑफ क्लेम प्रस्तुत कर प्रार्थी के कथनों का प्रतिवाद किया है कि प्रार्थी को दि. 18.1283 को मजदूर ई ग्रेम ऑपरेटिव 
केटेगरी में नियुक्ति दी गई थी। प्रार्थी हमेशा अपनी ड्यूटी से गैर हाजिर रहा था तथा अपनी ड्यूटी के प्रति लापरवाह था। इस कारण से प्रबंधक वर्ग 
द्वारा प्रार्थी को दि. 14.12.2004 को एक आरोप पत्र दिया गया। प्रबंधक वर्ग द्वारा कार्मिक को दि. 14.12.2004 का जो आरोप पत्र दिया गया था व 
आरोप लगाए गए थे उनकी विधिवत जांच प्राकृतिक न्याय के सिद्धांत को ध्यान में रखते हुए विभागीय जांच कराई गई थी जिसें प्रार्थी श्रमिक को 
सुनवाई का पूरा मौका दिया गया था। जांच अधिकारी ने श्रमिक पर लगाए गए आरोपों को सही पाया तथा जांच अधिकारी की रिपोर्ट के आधार पर 
श्रमिक की सेवाएं समाप्त की गई है। जांच अधिकारी ने जांच प्राकृतिक न्याय के प्रावधानों को ध्यान में रखते हुए की गई है। अगर न्यायालय इस 
निष्कर्ष पर पहूंचे कि विभागीय जांच में श्रमिक को सुनवाई का पूरा मौका नहीं दिया गया है तो अप्रार्थी विभागीय जांच न्यायालय में करवाने को तैयार

है। श्रमिक बिना छुट्टी का प्रार्थना पत्र दिए, बिना कोई सूचना fey बिना छुट्टी मंजूर कराए अपने कार्यस्थल से अनुपस्थित रहने की आदत सी बन 
गई है जिससे प्रबंधक को अपने संस्थान को चलाने में व प्रबंधन करने में काफी दिक्कत आती है। श्रमिक को इस बाबत काफी समझाया गया लेकिन 
श्रमिक अपनी आदत से बाज नहीं आता है। इस कारण से प्रबंधक को मजबूर हो कर श्रमिक के विरुद्ध उक्त विभागीय कार्यवाही करनी पड़ी जिसके 
लिए श्रमिक स्वयं जिम्मेदार है। श्रमिक ने दि. 01.122004 से 14.12.2004 तक की अनुपस्थिति का कारण अपनी पत्नि की तबीयत खराब होन बताया 
है लेकिन अपनी पत्नि की तबीयत खराब होने बाबत श्रमिक ने कोई डॉक्टर का प्रमाण पत्र कोई दस्तावेजी साक्ष्य आदि प्रस्तुत नहीं किए है। श्रमिक 
के विरुद्ध जो कार्यवाही की गई थी वो 2004 में की गईं थी श्रमिक ने जो विवाद उठाया है वो 2010 में उठाया है। इतने समय के पश्चात विवाद 
उठाने का कोई कारण श्रमिक ने नहीं बताया है ऐसी स्थिति में श्रमिक का विवाद चलने योग्य नहीं है। श्रमिक के विरूद्ध जो विभागीय जांच की गई 
है वो प्राकृतिक न्याय के सिद्धांतों को ध्यान में रखते हुए की गई है। श्रमिक को सुनवाई का पूरा मौका दिया गया है उसके पश्चात ही जांच 
अधिकारी ने अपनी विभागीय जांच में श्रमिक पर लगाए गए आरोपों को सही पाया गया है। श्रमिक ने विभागीय कार्य में जांच अधिकारी के समक्ष जो 
बयान दिए है उसमें श्रमिक ने उस पर लगाए गए आरोपों को स्वीकार किया है। विभागयी जांच के आधार पर ही श्रमिक की सेवाएं समाप्त की गई है 
और प्रार्थना की है कि उक्त आधार पर प्रार्थी का क्लेम प्रार्थना पत्र सव्यय खारिज किया जावे। 


5-यहाँ यह उल्लेखित किया जाना समीचीन होगा कि न्यायालय द्वारा दि.13.04.2022 को प्रार्थी श्रमिक के विरूद्ध की गयी घरेलु जाँच को नैसर्गिक 
न्याय के सिद्धांतों के अनुसार विधिसम्मत उचित होना माना गया है, ऐसे में अब उसमें दिये गये निष्कर्षों की पुनरावृत्ति की आवश्यकता नहीं है।

7-हस्तगत प्रकरण में न्यायालय को यह देखना है कि क्या प्रार्थी श्रमिक के विरुद्ध दिया गया दण्डादेश दि. 06.01.2005 उचित एवं वैध है अथवा नहीं 
और यदि उचित है तो कया दिया गया दण्ड उसके कृत्य की तुलना में समानक्षांत॥ (Proportionate) है अथवा नहीं? 

इस प्रकरण में प्रार्थी को आरोप पत्र दिनांक 14.12.2004 प्रार्थी के “दिनांक 01.12.2004 से आज दिनांक तक बिना पूर्व सूचना एवं बिना 
अवकाश स्वीकृत कराए अपने कार्य से अनाधिकृत रूप से अनुपस्थित” रहने बाबत दिया गया था। उक्त दोषारोपण का जवाब प्रस्तुत कर अनुपस्थित 
का कारण प्रार्थी ने बताया कि दि. 01.12.2004 से 15.12.2004 तक छुट्टी पर रहने का कारण उसकी पत्नी की तबीयत खराब होना था एवं वह 
विभाग में टाईम पर सूचना न करके छुट्टी पर चला गया। उक्त दोषारोपण का जवाब संतोषजनक नहीं पाए जाने के कारण अप्रार्थी द्वारा आरोप पत्र 
दि. 14.12.2004 की जांच के लिए नियोजक द्वारा एल.के.शर्मा, सीनियर ऑफिसर को जांच अधिकारी नियुक्ति किया गया है। 


दौराने विभागीय जांच नियोजक पक्ष की ओर से सुनिल कुमार तथा के.एस.दाधीच के बयान लेखबद्ध करवाए गए है और प्रार्थी ने भी अपनी प्रतिरक्षा 
में स्वयं के बयान लेखबद्ध करवाए हैं। पक्षकारान को ओर से प्रस्तुत साक्ष्य के आधार पर जांच अधिकारी ने आरोपी प्रार्थी के विरुद्ध लगे आरोप को 
प्रमाणित पाया है। उक्त आरोप के संबंध में नियोजक पक्ष की ओर से जांच में प्रस्तुत गवाहों सुनिल कुमार एवं के.एस.दाधीच ने अपनी गवाही में यह 
तथ्य बताया है कि वर्ष 2004 का में भैरूलाल की 12 Wave व 612 सी.एल. तथा 113/2 अनुपस्थित रहा तथा 4 दिन एल.डब्ल्यू पी. रहा है एवं बिना 
सूचना के दि. 01.12.2004 से अनुपस्थित था। 


प्रा्थी की ओर से दौराने विभागीय जांच स्वयं के बयान लेखबद्ध करवाए गए है एवं अपनी अनुपस्थिति की सूचना नहीं भेजने के तथ्य को 
स्वीकार किया है| 


8-यहां इस तथ्य को दृष्टिगत रखा जाना उचित होगा कि साक्ष्य दौराने अप्रार्थी के गवाहों के बयान मुख्य बिन्दुओं पर अखण्डित रहे है और प्रार्थी ने 
दौराने विभागीय जांच में हुए अपने बयान एवं आरोप पत्र के जवाब में में यह स्वीकार किया है कि वह बिना सूचना के दि. 01.12.2004 से दि. 15.12. 
2004 तक अनुपस्थित है एवं अप्रार्थी द्वारा प्रस्तुत की गई प्रार्थी की वर्ष 2004 की प्रफोर्मेंस रिपोर्ट से स्पष्ट है कि प्रार्थी वर्ष 2004 में 38 दिन सस्पेंड 
रहा है एवं अप्रार्थी द्वारा wet or विभागीय कार्यवाही का विवरण प्रस्तुत किया गया है जिसमें वह वर्ष 2000 से लेकर 2004 तक 20 बार बिना पूर्व 
सूचना के अनुपस्थित रहा है जिसमें उसे निलंबित किए जाने के दण्ड से दण्डित किया जा चुका है। इस प्रकार जांच अधिकारी ने पत्रावली पर 
उपलब्ध साक्ष्य के आधार पर प्रार्थी के विरूद्ध लगाए गए आरोपों को प्रमाणित पाया। इस प्रकार उपरोक्त विवेचन व विशलेषण से यह 
प्रकट हो रहा है कि इस प्रकरण में आरोपी प्रार्थी के विरुद्ध लगाए गए आरोप के संबंध A नियोजक पक्ष द्वारा 
अनुशासनात्मक कार्यवाही की गई और जांच अधिकारी द्वारा उक्त अनुशासनात्मक कार्यवाही में प्रार्थी आरोपी के 
विरुद्ध आरोप प्रमाणित पाया गया है एवं उक्त साक्ष्य के आधार पर प्रार्थी को आरोपीत आरोपों में दोषी पाए जाने का निष्कर्ष दिया गया 
है। जांच अधिकारी द्वारा प्रार्थी आरोपी के विरुद्ध लगाए गए आरोपों की विधि अनुसार जांच की गई, गवाहों के 
बयान लेखबद्ध कर प्रस्तुत साक्ष्य के आधार पर प्रार्थी आरोपी के fase लगाए गए आरोप को प्रमाणित पाया है

अधिकारी के समक्ष पेश किया गया है और ना ही इस न्यायालय में, जिससे कि प्रार्थी के कथनों पर विश्वास

आदतन बिना पूर्व सूचना दिए अनुपस्थित रहने का आदि है, अत: जांच के निष्कर्ष से यह न्यायालय भी सहमत

प्रेषित की गई है जिनके द्वारा उक्त जांच रिपोर्ट के आधार पर दण्डादेश पारित किया गया है। 
9-जहां तक दण्डादेश का प्रश्न है जांच अधिकारी द्वारा आरोप प्रमाणित पाए जाने पर अपनी रिपोर्ट फैक्द्री 
मैनेजर को प्रस्तुत की गई है तदुपरान्त फैक्ट्री मैनेजर द्वारा आरोपी प्रार्थी के बिना पूर्व सूचना के अनुपस्थिति के 
दुराचरण की परिस्थितियों पर विचार करने के पश्चात्‌ प्रार्थी आरोपी को दिनांक ०६.०१.२००५ से उसकी सेवाओं 
से बर्खास्त (Dismiss) किया गया है प्रकरण की परिस्थितियों को दृष्टिगत रखते हुए Gat आदेश किसी भी प्रकार 
से अननुपातिक (Disproportionate) अनुचित व अवैध नहीं है बल्कि पूर्णतया उचित एवं वैध है और उसमें न्यायालय की राय में किसी भी 
प्रकार से हस्तक्षेप किया जाना न्यायोचित नहीं है और प्रार्थी किसी अनुतोष का अधिकारी पाये जाने योग्य नहीं है एवं सम्प्रेषित निर्देश /रेफेन्स इसी 
अनुरूप उत्तरित होने योग्य है। 
परिणामस्वरूप भारत सरकार, श्रम मंत्रालय, नई दिल्ली द्वारा प्रासांगिक आदेश दिनांक 23.02.2012 के जरिये सम्प्रेषित निर्देश /विवाद को 
इसी अनुरूप उत्तरित किया जाता है कि प्रार्थी श्रमिक भैरू पुत्र पुरीलाल को अप्रार्थी नियोजक फैक्ट्री मैनेजर, मंगलम सीमेंट लि. द्वारा दिनांक 06.01. 
2005 द्वारा दिया गया सेवाओं से बर्खास्त (Dismiss) का दण्ड उचित एवं वैध है जिसमें किसी प्रकार से हस्तक्षेप या परिवर्तन की आवश्यकता 
नहीं है, फलस्वरूप प्रार्थी, अप्रार्थी से कोई अनुतोष प्राप्त करने का अधिकारी नहीं हैं। 
(संदीप कुमार शर्मा 
न्यायाधीश 
औद्योगिक न्यायाधिकरण(केन्द्रीय),कोटा(राज. 
अधिनिर्णय आज दिनांक 25.03.2025 को खुले न्यायाधिकरण में सुनाया जाकर हस्ताक्षरित किया गया जिसे नियमानुसार समुचित सरकार 
को प्रकाशनार्थ भिजवाया जावे। 


(संदीप कुमार शर्मा)

मेसर्स गुरदीप स्टोन TR के प्रबंधतंत्र के संबद्ध नियोजकों और उनके कर्मचारी के बीच अनुबंध में निर्दिष्ट केन्द्रीय सरकार 
औद्योगिक अधिकरण एवं श्रम न्‍्यायालय-1, धनबाद, पंचाट (रिफरेन्स न.-86/2004) को जैसा कि अनुलग्रक में दिखाया 
गया है, प्रकाशित करती है जो केन्द्रीय सरकार को सॉफ्ट कॉपी के साथ 08.09.2025 को प्राप्त हुआ था |

S.O. 1630.—In pursuance of Section 17 of the Industrial Dispute Act, 1947 (14 of 1947), the Central 
Government hereby publishes the award (Reference No. 86/2004) of the Central Government Industrial Tribunal 
cum Labour Court-1, Dhanbad as shown in the Annexure, in the Industrial dispute between the employers in 
relation to M/s Gurdeep Stone Works and Their workman which was received along with soft copy of the award 
by the Central Government on 08.09.2025. 


[No. L-29011/58/2004-IR(M] 

SALONI, Dy. Director 

ANNEXURE 
BEFORE THE CENTRAL GOVERNMENT INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL NO.1,DHANBAD 
In the matter of reference U/S 10 (1) (d)& (2A) of I.D.Act. 1947. 
Reference Case No. 86/2004 
Employer in relation to the management of M/s Gurdeep Stone Works, Pakur. 
AND. 


Their workman. 


Present: Shri Sachindra Kumar Pandey 


6040 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA :SEPTEMBER 13, 2025/BHADRA 22, 1947 [PART II—SEC. 3(ii)] 


Presiding Officer 
Appearances: 
For the Employers :- None. 
For the workman. :- None. 
State : Jharkhand. Industry:-Stone Works 
Dated 01/08/2025 
AWARD. 


In exercise of powers conferred under clause (d) of sub-section (1) and sub —section (2A) of Section 10 of 
the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 (14 of 1947),Government Of India through the Ministry of Labour, vide its Order 
No.L-2901 1/58/2004-(IR(M)) dated 16/08/2004 has been pleased to refer the following dispute between the employer 
1.6. management of M/s Gurdeep Stone Works, Pakur and their workman through the Talbaria Branch Secretary, 
Bihar Mines Quarry Workers Union, Pakur for adjudication by this Tribunal: 


“Whether the action of the management of M/s Gurdeep Stone Works, Pakur in terminating the 
services of Sh. Dulal Ghosh without complying Section 25-F of ID Act w.e.f. 01/04/03 is legal and or 
justified? If not, to what relief Sh. Ghosh is entitled?” 


2. On receiving order no. L-29011/58/2004-(IR(M)) dated 16/08/2004 Government of India, Ministry of Labour, 
New Delhi for adjudication of the dispute, Reference case no. 86 of 2004 was registered on 30.08.2004 and thereafter 
the notices were sent to the parties with a direction to appear and submit their written statements along with relevant 
documents in support of their claims and the witnesses. 


3. After service of notice, both parties appeared previously and filed their authorities, W/S and subsequently 
rejoinder. It further appears that after 2006 this case was put up after a long gap on 20.12.2021 and registered notices 
were issued to both the parties but neither the workman nor management appeared before this Tribunal. The 
registered notice sent to the both the parties returned with endorsement “Insufficient Address kindly provide Receiver 


Name” and “अधूरा पता रहने के कारण वापस किया गया”. It transpires from the case record that both the parties did not 


appear since the year 2021 which shows that they have lost their interest in this case and therefore, this Tribunal is of 
the opinion that the case deserves to be dismissed due to non prosecution. 


4. Hence, 
ORDERED 


that this case is hereby dismissed for non prosecution and a “No Dispute Award” be drawn up in respect of the 
above reference case. Let the copies of Award in duplicate be sent to the Ministry of Labour & Employment, 
Government of India, New Delhi for information and notification. 


SACHINDRA KUMAR PAndey, Presiding Officer 


नई दिल्‍ली, 8 सितम्बर, 2025

cum Labour Court, Bhubaneswar as shown in the Annexure, in the Industrial dispute between the employers in 


[भाग ए---खण्ड 361)] भारत का राजपत्र : सितम्बर 13, 2025/ भाद्र 22, 1947 6041 


relation to M/s Balda Block Iron Mines M/s Serajuddin & Co. and Shri Khetrabasi Parida which was received

AWARD 


In the present case, a reference was received from the Under Secretary to the Government of India, Ministry 
of Labour & Employment, New Delhi vide order No. L-26012/2/2018 — IR(M), dated 07.01.2019 under clause (d) of 
sub-section (1) and sub-section (2A) of Section 10 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 for adjudication of a dispute, 
under the following schedule:- 


“Whether the demand of Shri Khetrabasi Parida, Ex-Challan Clerk that his services have been terminated 
illegally w.e.f. 30.11.2011 by the management of Balda Balock Iron Mines of M/s. Serajuddin & Co. is legal 
& justified? If yes, what relief the workman is entitled to?” 


2. In the reference order, the Under Secretary to Government of India, Ministry of Labour & Employment, New 
Delhi commanded the parties raising the dispute to file statement of claim, complete with relevant documents, list of 
reliance and witnesses with this Tribunal within 15 days of receipt of the reference order and to forward a copy of 
such statement of claim to each one of the opposite parties involved _ in the dispute. 


3. The case of the 2" Party-Workman as per his statement of claim is as follows:- 


That, he was initially appointed on 01.01.2005 as Challan Clerkwith prescribed pay scale and posted at Balda Block 
Iron Ore Mines of the Management which is an industrial establishment functioning as per the Memorandum and 
Articles of Association of the company. However, all of a sudden on 30.11.2011 he was retrenched by the 


6042 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA :SEPTEMBER 13, 2025/BHADRA 22, 1947 [PART II—SEC. 3(ii)] 


Management from service without any written order and without any domestic enquiry conducted against him by the 
establishment. He had made representation before the Management for his reinstatement, and when no action was 
taken by the Management on his representation, he raised a dispute before the Regional Labour Commissioner 
(Central), Rourkela, and on failure of conciliation proceeding before the labour machinery, the present reference has 
been made. 


The 2" party-workman prays that a direction be given to the Management for his reinstatement in service with full 
back wages, compensation, cost and consequential service benefits. 


4. The 17 Party-Management has appeared and filed its written statement. The case of the 17 party- 
Management as per its written statement is as follows:- 


That the dispute raised by the 271 party-workman after eight years of cause of action is not maintainable. The 
Management had floated Voluntary Retirement Scheme and the 2™ party-workman had opted for Voluntary 
Retirement Scheme with acceptance of all dues under the Scheme, as such, the present reference is also not 
maintainable. In order to down-size the establishment the Management had floated a V.R. Scheme for all the 
employees including regular and daily wage employees and in pursuance to the same the 2™ party-workman 
submitted application on 12.11.2011 to avail V.R. Scheme. The application of the applicant was considered and a 
total V.R. amount of Rs. 4,29,858/- was deposited on 28.08.2012 in his Bank Account and also an amount of Rs. 
13,138/- was paid towards Bonus to the workman in his Bank Account. In view of acceptance of V.R. Scheme 79५ 
the 2" party-workman his allegation of illegal termination by the Management on 30.11.2011 is false and 
consequently the reference is not maintainable. 


The 1“ Party-Management has prayed that the statement of claim of the 21 party-workman may be dismissed as not 
maintainable. 


5. When the case was fixed for filing of rejoinder by the 2" Party-workman and for framing of issues, the 
277 party-workman remained absent and did not participate in the proceedings. It is also revealed from the order-sheet 
that the 27 Party-workman has not appeared and remained absent in all the dates after filing of written statement by 
the Management. However, despite providing a number of opportunities, 27 Party-workman has not turned up to file 
rejoinder and to prove his case. As the 2™ party-workman has not turned up for proving his case, an order of 
exparte was passed against the 27 party-workman on dated 02.01.2024. 


6. The 17 Party-Management has examined one witness namely Sri Anjan Kumar Sahoo as M.W.-1 and he has 
proved certain documents which are as follows:- 


Ext.-1 is the copy of an Office Order dated 14.05.2011 for availing of VR Scheme floated by the Management. 
Ext.-2 is the copy of an application of the concerned workman for availing of V.R. Scheme. 

Ext.-3 is the copy of the Bank Statement showing payment of V.R.S. amount Rs. 4,29,858/- and Bonus Rs. 13,138/-. 
Ext.-4 is the copy of the reply filed by the Management before the R.L..C© Rourkela. 


7. The Management Witness No. | Sri Anjan Kumar Sahoo has deposed that the management had floated a 
Voluntary Retirement Scheme and the workman had submitted an application for availing V.R. Scheme which was 
accepted by the Management. He has also deposed that the concerned workman was paid Rs. 4,29,858/- towards 
V.R.S amount and Bonus Rs. 13,138/-, so all the dues had been paid to the 2™ party-workman under V.R.S. Scheme 
and the case is not maintainable. In the cross examination of M.W.-1 by Court he has stated that the 2" party- 
workman was working since 2006 under the Management and there was no minimum period prescribed for taking 
VRS. 


8. It appears from the record that the 27 party workman was given ample opportunity to appear before this 
Tribunal but he failed to do so. 


9. After analysing all the oral and documentary evidence of the 17 Party-Management the Tribunal finds that 
the 2™ party-workman had availed voluntary retirement scheme and had subsequently received the amount of Rs. 
4,29,858/- under V.R. Scheme and Bonus Rs. 13,138/- by the 1“ Party-Management so the 20 party-workman was 
not pursuing this case. 


10. In view of the such the Tribunal comes to conclusion that the services of the 2" party-workman has not been 
illegally terminated by the management rather he had accepted voluntary retirement under V.R. Scheme floated by the 
Management. Hence he is not entitled for any relief. 


11. Order is passed accordingly. 


12. A copy of this Order is sent to the appropriate government for notification as required under section 17 of the 
I.D. Act, 1947. File is consigned to record room. 


[भाग Ive 361)] भारत का राजपत्र : सितम्बर 13, 2025/ भाद्र 22, 1947 6043 


Dictated & Corrected by me. 


DINESH KUMAR SINGH, Presiding Officer

In the present case, a reference was received from the Under Secretary to the Government of India, Ministry

under the following schedule:- 


“Whether the termination of Shri Rajat Kumar Sethy, Ex-Security Guard w.e.f. 30.11.2011 by the 
management of Balda Block Iron mines of M/s. Serajuddin & Co. is proper and legally justified? If no, what relief 
Shri Rajat Kumar Sethy is entitled to? What directions, if any, are necessary in the matter?” 


2. In the reference order, the Under Secretary to Government of India, Ministry of Labour & Employment, New 
Delhi commanded the parties raising the dispute to file statement of claim, complete with relevant documents, list of 
reliance and witnesses with this Tribunal within 15 days of receipt of the reference order and to forward a copy of 
such statement of claim to each one of the opposite parties involved in the dispute. 


3. The case of the 277 Party-Workman as per his statement of claim is as follows:- 


That, he was initially appointed on 01.03.2004 as Security Guard (Gunman) with prescribed pay scale and 
posted at Balda Block Iron Ore Mines of the Management which is an industrial establishment functioning as per the 
Memorandum and Articles of Association of the company. However, all of a sudden on 30.11.2011 he was retrenched 
by the Management from service without any written order and without any domestic enquiry conducted against him 
by the establishment. He had made representation before the Management for his _ reinstatement, and when no action 
was taken by the Management on his representation, he raised a dispute before the Regional Labour Commissioner 
(Central), Rourkela, and on failure of conciliation proceeding before the labour machinery, the present reference has 
been made. 


The 21 party-workman prays that a direction be given to the Management for his reinstatement in service 
with full back wages, compensation, cost and consequential service benefits. 


4. The 17 Party-Management has appeared and filed its written statement. The case of the 17 party- 
Management as per its written statement is as follows:- 


That the dispute raised by the 2" party-workman after eight years of cause of action is not maintainable. 
The Management had floated Voluntary Retirement Scheme and the 20 party-workman had opted for Voluntary

maintainable. In order to down-size the establishment the | Management had floated a V.R. Scheme for all the 
employees including regular and daily wage employees and in pursuance to the same the 2™ party-workman 
submitted application on 26.08.2011 to avail V.R. Scheme. The application of the applicant was considered and a 
total V.R. amount of Rs. 4,08,212/- was deposited on 31.10.2011 in his Bank Account and also an amount of Rs. 
8,954/- was paid towards Bonus to the workman in his Bank Account. In view of acceptance of V.R. Scheme by the 
277 party-workman his allegation of illegal termination by the Management on 30.11.2011 is false and consequently 
the reference is not maintainable. 


The 11 Party-Management has prayed that the statement of claim of the 27 party-workman may be dismissed 
as not maintainable.

party-workman remained absent and did not participate in the proceedings. It is also revealed from the order-sheet 
that the 2™ Party-workman has not appeared and remained absent in all the dates after filing of written statement by 
the Management. However, despite providing a number of opportunities, 


277 Party-workman has not turned up to file rejoinder and to prove his case. As the 27 party-workman has not turned 
up for proving his case, an order of exparte was passed against the 27 party-workman on dated 02.01.2024. 


6. The 17 Party-Management has examined one witness namely Sri Anjan Kumar Sahoo as M.W.-1 and he has 
proved certain documents which are as follows:- 


Ext.-1 is the copy of an Office Order dated 14.05.2011 for availing of VR Scheme floated by the Management. 
Ext.-2 is the copy of an application of the concerned workman for availing of V.R. Scheme. 

Ext.-3 is the copy of the Bank Statement showing payment of V.R.S. amount Rs. 4,08,212/- and Bonus Rs. 8,954/-. 
Ext.-4 is the copy of the reply filed by the Management before the R.L..C© Rourkela. 


Ts The Management Witness No. | Sri Anjan Kumar Sahoo has deposed that the management had floated a 
Voluntary Retirement Scheme and the workman had submitted an application for availing V.R. Scheme which was 
accepted by the Management. He has also deposed that the concerned workman was paid Rs. 4,08,212/- towards 
V.R.S amount and Bonus Rs. 8,954/-, so all the dues had been paid to the 2™ party-workman under V.R.S. Scheme 
and the case is not maintainable. In the cross examination of M.W.-1 by Court he has stated that the 2™ party- 
workman was working since 2006 under the Management and there was no minimum period prescribed for 
taking VRS. 


[भाग ए---खण्ड 361)] भारत का राजपत्र : सितम्बर 13, 2025/ भाद्र 22, 1947 6045 


8. It appears from the record that the 2™ party workman was given ample opportunity to appear before this 
Tribunal but he failed to do so. 
9. After analysing all the oral and documentary evidence of the 17 Party-Management the Tribunal finds that 


the 214 party-workman had availed voluntary retirement scheme and had subsequently received the amount of Rs. 
4,08,212/- under V.R. Scheme and Bonus Rs. 8,954/- by the 1“ Party-Management so the 27 party-workman was not 
pursuing this case. 


10. In view of the such the Tribunal comes to conclusion that the services of the 27 party-workman has not been 
illegally terminated by the management rather he had accepted voluntary retirement under V.R. Scheme floated by 
the Management. Hence he is not entitled for any relief.

मेसर्स बलदा ब्लॉक आयरन माइंस मेसर्स सेराजुद्दीन एंड कंपनी के प्रबंधतंत्र के संबद्ध नियोजकों और मोहम्मद सौकत 
अल्लाम के बीच अनुबंध में निर्दिष्ट केन्द्रीय सरकार औद्योगिक अधिकरण एवं श्रम न्यायालय, भुवनेश्वर, पंचाट (रिफरेन्स 
न.-10/2019) को जैसा कि अनुलग्रक में दिखाया गया है, प्रकाशित करती है जो केन्द्रीय सरकार को सॉफ्ट कॉपी के साथ 
08.09.2025 को प्राप्त हुआ था | 


[सं, एल-26012/4/2018-आईआर(एम)]

6046 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA :SEPTEMBER 13, 2025/BHADRA 22, 1947 [PART II—SEC. 3(ii)] 


Advocate. Management. 
None. ek For the 277 party- 
Workmen. 
AWARD 


In the present case, a reference was received from the Under Secretary to the Government of India, Ministry 
of Labour & Employment, New Delhi vide order No. L-26012/4/2018 — IR(M), dated 24.01.2019 under clause (d) of 
sub-section (1) and sub-section (2A) of Section 10 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 for adjudication of a dispute, 
under the following schedule:- 

“Whether the termination of the services of Md. Saukat Allam, Ex-Driver w.e.f. 30.11.2011 by the management 
of Balda Block Iron mines of M/s. Serajuddin & Co. is proper and legally justified? If no, what relief Md. Saukat 
Allam is entitled to? What directions, if any, are necessary in the matter?” 
2s In the reference order, the Under Secretary to Government of India, Ministry of Labour & Employment, New 
Delhi commanded the parties raising the dispute to file statement of claim, complete with relevant documents, list of 
reliance and witnesses with this Tribunal within 15 days of receipt of the reference order and to forward a copy of 
such statement of claim to each one of the opposite parties involved in the dispute. 

3. The case of the 277 Party-Workman as per his statement of claim is as follows:- 


That, he was initially appointed on 01.01.2004 as Driver with prescribed pay scale and posted at Balda Block 
Iron Ore Mines of the Management which is an industrial establishment functioning as per the Memorandum and 
Articles of Association of the company. However, all of a sudden on 30.11.2011 he was retrenched by the 
Management from service without any written order and without any domestic enquiry conducted against him by the 
establishment. He had made representation before the Management for his reinstatement, and when no action was 
taken by the Management on his representation, he raised a dispute before the Regional Labour Commissioner 
(Central), Rourkela, and on failure of conciliation proceeding before the labour machinery, the present reference has 
been made. 

The 21 party-workman prays that a direction be given to the Management for his reinstatement in service 
with full back wages, compensation, cost and consequential service benefits. 

4. The 17 Party-Management has appeared and filed its written statement. The case of the 11 party- 
Management as per its written statement is as follows:- 

That the dispute raised by the 2 party-workman after eight years of cause of action is not maintainable. The 
Management had floated Voluntary Retirement Scheme and the 2™ party-workman had opted for Voluntary 
Retirement Scheme with acceptance of all dues under the Scheme, as such, the present reference is also not 
maintainable. In order to down-size the establishment the | Management had floated a V.R. Scheme for all the 
employees including regular and daily wage employees and in pursuance to the same the 2™ party-workman 
submitted application to avail V.R. Scheme. The application of the applicant was considered and a total V.R. amount 
of Rs. 4,64,994/- was deposited on 28.08.2012 in his Bank Account and also an amount of Rs. 13,138/- was paid 
towards Bonus to the workman in his Bank Account. In view of acceptance of V.R. Scheme by the 277 party-workman 
his allegation of illegal termination by the Management on 30.11.2011 is false and consequently the reference is not 
maintainable. 

The 1‘ Party-Management has prayed that the statement of claim of the 2" party-workman may be dismissed 
as not maintainable. 
ay When the case was fixed for filing of rejoinder by the 2" Party-workman and for framing of issues, the 2™ 
party-workman remained absent and did not participate in the proceedings. It is also revealed from the order-sheet 
that the 27 Party-workman has not appeared and remained absent in all the dates after filing of written statement by 
the Management. However, despite providing a number of opportunities, 27 Party-workman has not turned up to file 
rejoinder and to prove his case. As the 2™ party-workman has not turned up for proving his case, 
an order of exparte was passed against the 2™ party-workman on dated 02.01.2024. 

6. The 17 Party-Management has examined one witness namely Sri Anjan Kumar Sahoo as M.W.-1 and he has 
proved certain documents which are as follows:- 

Ext.-1 is the copy of an Office Order dated 14.05.2011 for availing of VR Scheme floated by the Management. 

Ext.-2 is the copy of an application of the concerned workman for availing of V.R. Scheme. 

Ext.-3 is the copy of the Bank Statement showing payment of V.R.S. amount Rs. 4,64,994/- and Bonus Rs. 13,138/-. 
Ext.-4 is the copy of the reply filed by the Management before the R.L..C© Rourkela. 

Ts The Management Witness No. | Sri Anjan Kumar Sahoo has deposed that the management had floated a 
Voluntary Retirement Scheme and the workman had submitted an application for availing V.R. Scheme which was 
accepted by the Management. He has also deposed that the concerned workman was paid Rs. 4,64,994/- towards 
V.R.S amount and Bonus Rs. 13,138/-, so all the dues had been paid to the 2™ party-workman under V.R.S. Scheme 


[भाग ए---खण्ड 361)] भारत का राजपत्र : सितम्बर 13, 2025/ भाद्र 22, 1947 6047 


and the case is not maintainable. In the cross examination of M.W.-1 by Court he has stated that the 27 party- 
workman was working since 2006 under the Management and there was no minimum period prescribed for taking 
VRS. 


8. It appears from the record that the 27 party workman was given ample opportunity to appear before this 
Tribunal but he failed to do so. 


9. After analysing all the oral and documentary evidence of the 17 Party-Management the Tribunal finds that 
the 2™ party-workman had availed voluntary retirement scheme and had subsequently received 


the amount of Rs. 4,64,994/- under V.R. Scheme and Bonus Rs. 13,138/- by the 1“ Party-Management so the 271 
party-workman was not pursuing this case. 


10. In view of the such the Tribunal comes to conclusion that the services of the 27 party-workman has not been 
illegally terminated by the management rather he had accepted voluntary retirement under V.R. Scheme floated by 
the Management. Hence he is not entitled for any relief. 


11. Award is passed accordingly. 


12. A copy of this Award is sent to the appropriate government for notification as required under section 17 of 
the I.D. Act, 1947. File is consigned to record room. 


Dictated & Corrected by me. 


Presiding Officer, CGIT-cum-Labour Court, BBSR. 
नई दिल्ली, 8 सितम्बर, 2025

“Whether the termination of Shri Siba Charan Parida, Ex-L.M.V. Operator w.e.f. 30.11.2011 by the management of 
Balda Block Iron mines of M/s. Serajuddin & Co. is proper and legally justified? If no, what relief Shri Siba Charan 
Parida is entitled to? What directions, if any, are necessary in the matter?” 
2. In the reference order, the Under Secretary to Government of India, Ministry of Labour & Employment, 
New Delhi commanded the parties raising the dispute to file statement of claim, complete with relevant documents, list 
of reliance and witnesses with this Tribunal within 15 days of receipt of the reference order and to forward a copy of 
such statement of claim to each one of the opposite parties involved in the dispute. 
3. The case of the 277 Party-Workman as per his statement of claim is as follows:- 
That, he was initially appointed on 01.01.2000 as L.M.V. Operator with prescribed pay scale and posted at Balda Block 
Iron Ore Mines of the Management which is an industrial establishment functioning as per the Memorandum and 
Articles of Association of the company. However, all of a sudden on 30.11.2011 he was retrenched by the Management 
from service without any written order and without any domestic enquiry conducted against him by the establishment. 
He had made representation before the Management for his reinstatement, and when no action was taken by the 
Management on his representation, he raised a dispute before the Regional Labour Commissioner (Central), Rourkela, 
and on failure of conciliation proceeding before the labour machinery, the present reference has been made. 
The 27 party-workman prays that a direction be given to the Management for his reinstatement in service with full 
back wages, compensation, cost and consequential service benefits. 
4. The 17 Party-Management has appeared and filed its written statement. The case of the 1“ party-Management 
as per its written statement is as follows:- 
That the dispute raised by the 2" party-workman after eight years of cause of action is not maintainable. The

Scheme with acceptance of all dues under the Scheme, as such, the present reference is also not maintainable. In order 
to down-size the establishment the Management had floated a V.R. Scheme for all the employees including regular and 
daily wage employees and in pursuance to the same the 2™ party-workman submitted application on 12.11.2011 to 
avail V.R. Scheme. The application of the applicant was considered and a total V.R. amount of Rs. 4,56,426/- was 
deposited on 28.08.2012 in his Bank Account and also an amount of Rs. 13,138/- was paid towards Bonus to the 
workman on 09.10.2013 in his Bank Account. In view of acceptance of V.R. Scheme by the 2™ party-workman his 
allegation of illegal termination by the Management on 30.11.2011 is false and consequently the reference is not 
maintainable. 
The 1" Party-Management has prayed that the statement of claim of the 2™ party-workman may be dismissed as not 
maintainable.

277 party-workman remained absent and did not participate in the proceedings. It is also revealed from the order-sheet 
that the 271 Party-workman has not appeared and remained absent in all the dates after filing of written statement by the 


[भाग ए---खण्ड 361)] भारत का राजपत्र : सितम्बर 13, 2025/ भाद्र 22, 1947 6049 


Management. However, despite providing a number of opportunities, 2"' Party-workman has not turned up to file

accepted by the Management. He has also deposed that the concerned workman was paid Rs. 4,56,426/- towards V.R.S 
amount and Bonus Rs. 13,138/-, so all the dues had been paid to the 27 party-workman under V.R.S. Scheme and the 
case is not maintainable. In the cross examination of M.W.-1 by Court he has stated that the 2" party-workman was

2" party-workman had availed voluntary retirement scheme and had subsequently received 


the amount of Rs. 4,56,426/- under V.R. Scheme and Bonus Rs. 13,138/- by the 1“ Party-Management so the 2™ party- 
workman was not pursuing this case.

reliance and witnesses with this Tribunal within 15 days of receipt of the reference order and to forward a copy of 
such statement of claim to each one of the opposite parties involved in the dispute.

सरकार ईएसआई; मेसर्स हाइब्रिड फ्लीट मैनेजमेंट प्राइवेट लिमिटेड के प्रबंधतंत्र के संबद्ध नियोजकों और सुश्री रश्मि पांडे
